### [183] [Bridging the Epistemic Gap in the Invisible Internet: Extended Mathematical Modeling and Empirical Characterization of I2P Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23825)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Jacques Bou Abdo,Liaquat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过从生产代码中推导并验证的扩展数学模型（EMM），揭示了I2P网络的真实拓扑特征：路由器可见性有限（~25%）、分层分配相对性、Fast层隧道偏好及High Capacity层探索隧道过度代表，为网络威慑与安全设计提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于协议文档的模型无法捕捉I2P网络的真实行为，亟需更精确的模型来理解其全局拓扑结构与安全特性。

Method: 从生产代码推导扩展数学模型（EMM），在六路由器、地理分布测试床上进行30天验证实验。

Result: 路由器可见性约25%；层级分配呈相对性；Fast层隧道偏好显著；High Capacity层探索隧道过度代表；模型能准确预测拓扑不对称性及破坏隐私所需的对抗资源。

Conclusion: 该模型为网络威慑和安全的去中心化网络设计提供了原理性基础。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) is a decentralized, peer-to-peer network underpinning secure and adversarial communications with major implications for cybersecurity and national security. Its global topology emerges from thousands of independent peer-selection decisions, producing a hub-dominated, complex structure that resists external observation. Prior models based on protocol documentation failed to capture real-world behavior. We close this gap with an Extended Mathematical Model (EMM) derived from production code and validated on a six-router, geographically distributed testbed over thirty days. Results reveal limited router visibility (~25% of nodes), relativistic tier allocations, strong Fast-tier tunnel preference, and overrepresented High Capacity-tier exploratory tunnels. The model accurately predicts emergent topological asymmetries and defines the adversarial resources required to compromise privacy, providing a principled foundation for cyber deterrence and secure decentralized network design.

</details>


### [184] [TriShieldRAG: A Three-Ring Defense-in-Depth Framework Against Knowledge Corruption in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23838)
*Susil Kumar Mohanty,Rohit Patel,Kosuru Yuvaraj,Jeenal Chaudhary,Disha Singhania*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriShieldRAG通过三道防线（Ingest Guard、Retrieval Scorer、Cross-LLM Consensus）将RAG系统的攻击成功率从约91%降至约13%，同时保持良性查询的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部知识库检索结果，当知识库接受多方写入时，少量对抗性文档即可操纵模型输出错误答案（PoisonedRAG展示仅5篇对抗文档即可使无防御系统约90%被攻击），现有防御方案（困惑度过滤、查询改写、知识库扩展）攻击成功率仍高于30%，亟需更有效的防御方法。

Method: 提出TriShieldRAG三重防御架构：1) Ingest Guard：在文档摄入时检测词汇和统计层面的投毒特征；2) Retrieval Scorer：基于来源一致性和一致性加权信任分数对检索结果重新排序；3) Cross-LLM Consensus：投票三种不同架构的大语言模型（Claude、Mistral Small、Llama 3.2），在意见不一致时允许一次有限的重检索。

Result: 在5000篇Wikipedia知识库、10个目标问题的评估中，针对PoisonedRAG的非自适应攻击者，攻击成功率从约91%降至约13%，同时良性查询准确率保持不变。

Conclusion: TriShieldRAG通过形式上严格定义的多重防御机制，有效弥补了RAG系统的安全漏洞，显著降低了对抗性文档投毒攻击的成功率，同时不影响正常查询的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets a large language model answer questions using documents retrieved from an external knowledge base at query time. This makes RAG useful for private data, fast-changing information, and reducing hallucination, but it also means the model's answer is only as trustworthy as whatever the retriever hands it. If the knowledge base accepts writes from more than one party, an attacker needs only a handful of adversarial documents to steer the model toward a chosen wrong answer. PoisonedRAG demonstrated this: as few as five crafted documents flip an undefended system's answer roughly 90% of the time, and three natural single-stage defenses (perplexity filtering, query paraphrasing, knowledge-base expansion) leave attack success at 30% or higher.
  We built TriShieldRAG to close that gap. Rather than relying on one checkpoint, we place three independent, formally specified rings across the pipeline: an Ingest Guard that screens documents for lexical and statistical poisoning signatures; a Retrieval Scorer that re-ranks the retrieved set by a provenance and consistency-weighted trust score; and a Cross-LLM Consensus stage that polls three architecturally diverse language models (Claude, Mistral Small, Llama 3.2) and allows one bounded re-retrieval on disagreement.
  We derive the conditions under which Rings 2 and 3 are expected to work: a minority-poison assumption and an explicit provenance-tag assumption. Our reported configuration is consistent with this analysis, though we have not yet run the controlled poison-fraction sweep needed to confirm it independently. Evaluated against the non-adaptive attacker from the original PoisonedRAG, over a 5,000-document Wikipedia knowledge base with 10 target questions, the full pipeline reduces attack success rate from roughly 91% to roughly 13% while preserving accuracy on benign queries.

</details>


### [185] [On a necessary condition for the matching cryptosystem stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23843)
*Aleksey Bolotnikov,Anwar Irmatov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文描述了一种针对匹配密码系统的攻击及其有限噪声防御方案。公钥包含图和边权重向量函数，私钥是另一种可多项式求解交替加权路径问题的权重函数。通过分析公钥中特定边集的权重向量张成空间的维度，提出了匹配密码系统稳定性的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究匹配密码系统的安全性，提出一种可能的攻击方式，并设计相应的防御机制（有限噪声），以增强密码系统的稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种基于有限噪声的防御方案，考虑私钥权重函数的特定族，其中部分向量值坐标被描述为有限噪声；通过分析公钥图中特定边集的权重向量张成空间的维度，给出了匹配密码系统稳定性的必要条件。

Result: 给出了匹配密码系统稳定性的一个必要条件，该条件与公钥图中特定边集的权重向量张成空间的维度相关。

Conclusion: 通过引入有限噪声和维度条件分析，为匹配密码系统的安全性评估提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The article contains a description of a possible attack on a matching cryptosystem and a defense with limited noise. A public key of a matching cryposystem consists of a graph and a weight vector-function on the edges of the graph with values from a finite field, where a private key contains another weight function, for which the corresponding alternating weighted path problem can be solved in polynomial time. There is a specific family of these secret weight functions that is considered in this article, for which some of the coordinates of its vector values are described as limited noise. We suggest a necessary condition for the matching cryptosystem stability in terms of dimensions of spans of weight vectors that correspond to specific sets of edges of the graph from the public key.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond GDPR: Examining Disclosure Gaps in Mobile AR Privacy Policies under U.S. State Privacy Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23984)
*Hong Chen,Xueling Zhang,Hong-Ning Dai,Huashan Chen,Qin Yu,Tiange Xie,Duohe Ma,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模审计了美国州级隐私法下的移动增强现实（MAR）应用隐私政策，发现44.62%的政策存在严重披露缺失，部分条款违规率超90%。研究构建了包含8,013个应用元数据、6,426份隐私政策文件的数据集，并开发了自动化审计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAR应用可收集空间地图、生物特征等高敏感数据，但现有隐私政策研究多聚焦GDPR等单一法律框架，忽视了美国20个州综合隐私法形成的碎片化监管环境，缺乏针对州级立法的系统性审计。

Method: 构建涵盖全球8,013个Google Play MAR应用元数据及美国6,620个APK、6,426份隐私政策的数据集；推导出包含5项基线要求、10项触发要求和4条逻辑链的可审计披露分类体系；开发并验证了四阶段自动化审计管线，能生成可追溯、有证据支撑的披露判断。

Result: 44.62%的审计政策存在严重披露缺失（缺失超过8项要求），4项隐私政策要求的违规率超过90%，表明MAR隐私披露远未跟上美国州级隐私法规的复杂化进程。

Conclusion: MAR应用的隐私披露存在广泛且严重的合规缺口，现有实践无法满足日益复杂的州级隐私监管要求。研究公开了数据集、分类体系和审计管线，以促进可扩展的隐私合规审计研究。

Abstract: Mobile Augmented Reality (MAR) apps can collect and process highly sensitive data such as spatial maps and biometrics, yet their privacy policies remain largely understudied. Prior audits of app privacy policies have typically focused on a single legal framework, such as the GDPR. Meanwhile, 20 U.S. states have comprehensive privacy laws in effect, creating a fragmented and rapidly evolving set of privacy policy obligations. To date, no study has systematically audited privacy policies against this emerging body of state-level legislation.
  In this paper, we present the first large-scale audit of MAR privacy policies under U.S. state privacy laws. We construct a dataset covering the MAR ecosystem, including 8,013 Google Play MAR app metadata records worldwide, and a U.S.-based subset with 6,620 APKs and 6,426 privacy policy files. We further derive an auditable disclosure taxonomy with 5 baseline requirements, 10 triggered requirements, and 4 logic chains, and build a validated four-stage automated pipeline that produces traceable, evidence-grounded disclosure judgments.
  Our audit reveals widespread disclosure gaps: 44.62\% of audited policies exhibit severe disclosure omissions, with each missing more than eight requirements, and four privacy-policy requirements have violation rates above 90\%. These findings suggest that MAR privacy disclosures are not keeping pace with the growing complexity of U.S. state privacy regulation. We release our dataset, taxonomy, and auditing pipeline to support future research on scalable privacy compliance auditing.

</details>


### [187] [ContainmentBench: Trace-Based Evaluation of Post-Injection Containment in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23999)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Xinhua Lai,Meiqi Wu,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有提示注入安全评估仅关注终端策略结果，但相同终点可能掩盖不同的传播痕迹和效用损失。ContainmentBench通过追踪记录、恢复检测和授权效用三个维度，发现仅73.5%的案例在轨迹或效用上存在差异，且不同防护策略下终端策略相同但授权工作流完成率差异巨大（0.1642 vs 0.8567 vs 0.9233），证明终端策略标签不能充分反映操作层面的安全状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入安全评估仅用终端攻击或策略结果总结安全性，忽略了相同的端点结果可能掩盖不同的暴露后传播痕迹和授权效用损失，导致安全评估不充分。

Method: 提出ContainmentBench基准，构建沙箱追踪式评估框架，分别测量端点策略合规性、传播日志、恢复检测和结构化授权行为完成度，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上进行了17,640次回放实验。

Result: 600对匹配的主动污染对中，所有对均零危害，但73.5%在传播轨迹或效用上不同；仅污染策略完成0.1642授权工作流，可信账本策略提高到0.8567，强工具边界基线达到0.9233，且传播排名随证据阶段和分母选择变化。

Conclusion: 终端策略标签不能充分反映操作层面的暴露后安全状态；评估应分别报告端点、阶段分层轨迹和效用证据，仅当对应控制有效时才将恢复证据纳入比较。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents process untrusted content, maintain memory, delegate across agents, and invoke side-effecting tools. Existing prompt-injection evaluations typically summarize security with terminal attack or policy outcomes, but equal endpoints can conceal different post-exposure traces and different losses of authorized utility. We introduce ContainmentBench, a sandboxed, trace-based benchmark that separately measures benchmark-defined endpoint policy compliance, instrumented logged propagation, recovery instrumentation, and authorized structured-action completion. In a pre-specified 17,640-rollout study with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, all 600 matched active-tainted pairs comparing taint-only and intent-aware enforcement have the same zero committed-harm outcome, yet 73.5% differ in logged trajectory or utility. Taint-only enforcement completes only 0.1642 of authorized tainted workflows; a trusted-ledger policy raises completion to 0.8567, while a strong tool-boundary baseline reaches 0.9233 under the same observed endpoint-policy outcomes. We also find that aggregate logged-spread rankings change with evidence-stage composition and denominator choice. These results show that a terminal policy label is not a sufficient statistic for operational post-exposure containment; evaluations should report endpoint, stage-stratified trajectory, and utility evidence separately, and should promote recovery evidence to comparative claims only where the corresponding controls are valid. The full-scale study is synthetic and single-model; the policy case additionally assumes a correct structured authorization ledger.

</details>


### [188] [Agentic Cloud Decoys: A Deception-Driven Framework for Autonomous Intrusion Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24006)
*Mohan Manivannan,Dalal Alharthi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cloud Decoy AI Agent框架，将高保真云蜜罐与自主语言模型代理结合，自动将可疑云活动压缩为分析师可读报告。针对会话聚合、证据边界和对抗性遥测注入三大挑战，提出了会话聚合算子和动态提示生成方法，并在AWS S3场景下取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云遥测数据规模庞大，攻击者通过合法凭证、联邦令牌和云原生API进行攻击，与正常管理行为难以区分。分析师需要花费大量时间从日志中重建上下文，现有方法效率低下。

Method: 1) 高保真云蜜罐与LLM代理配对；2) 基于提供者派生字段的会话聚合算子；3) 两阶段动态提示生成，维护grounding不变性；4) 识别了对抗性遥测注入（间接提示注入）问题。

Result: 在10个AWS S3场景中，9个被完全重建，报告中所有断言均可追溯至观测到的工件，延迟为4-5分钟。

Conclusion: Cloud Decoy AI Agent能有效压缩从可疑活动到分析师报告的路径，但对抗性遥测注入问题尚未解决，需要后续缓解措施。

Abstract: Cloud telemetry arrives at a scale that, paradoxically, makes intrusion understanding harder rather than easier. Attackers operate through legitimate identity, federated session tokens, and cloud native APIs indistinguishable from routine administration, and analysts spend an incident reconstructing context the logs already contain. We present Cloud Decoy AI Agent, a framework pairing a high fidelity cloud decoy with an autonomous language model agent that compresses the path from suspicious activity to an analyst ready report. Connecting a decoy to an agent is not a wiring exercise. The unit of investigation is the session rather than the event, and the session key is obscured by the identity layering federated credentials introduce. The agent's evidence horizon must be bounded, since an agent free to query full control plane history inherits the cost and false positive profile deception was meant to remove. And cloud telemetry is partly adversary authored, since object keys and user agent strings are attacker chosen values providers record verbatim, which makes any log to prompt path an indirect prompt injection channel that a decoy widens rather than narrows. We address the first two with a session aggregation operator over a pivot tuple drawn only from provider derived fields, and with dynamic prompt generation, a two stage prompt assembly enforcing a grounding invariant by carrying only fields the agent observed. We identify the third as an unaddressed exposure in this class of system, specify the mitigation it requires, and note our prototype does not implement it. Across ten controlled AWS S3 scenarios, nine were reconstructed completely, no report contained an assertion untraceable to an observed artifact, and latency was four to five minutes. We also state what this evaluation does not establish and name the comparisons that would settle it.

</details>


### [189] [A Cybersecurity MLPS Large Language Model with Multi-Path Retrieval Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24116)
*Qian Li,Zhenyan Qi,Liang Shen,Yuan Zhang,Yifan Wan,Junyuan Ma,Yining Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向等保（MLPS）的领域大语言模型框架，通过集成层级检索、树状检索和分词匹配检索等多种策略，解决了通用大模型在标准密集和安全敏感场景下推理不可控、规则理解不完整的问题。实验证明该模型在十个典型问题上获得了更高的综合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前等保分析主要依赖人工解读和规则工具，在复杂应用场景下难以提供稳定一致的合规分析，迫切需要更智能化的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个集成多种检索策略的大语言模型框架：结合层级检索（Hierarchical Retrieval）、树状检索（Tree-based Retrieval）和基于分词匹配的检索（Tokenization-based Matching Retrieval），并采用多维度加权评分方法对模型回答进行定量评估。

Result: 在十个典型问题的对比实验中，所提出的领域专用大语言模型在整体评分上优于通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保持检索覆盖范围的同时减少无关上下文对推理过程的干扰，在等保问答的条款准确性、结论可追溯性和实际可部署性方面表现更优。

Abstract: The Multi-Level Protection Scheme (MLPS) is a foundational system in China's cybersecurity governance framework. Therefore, accurate analysis and understanding of MLPS requirements are essential. At present, MLPS analysis still relies mainly on manual interpretation of standards and rule-based tools. This makes it hard to provide stable and consistent compliance analysis in complex application scenarios. The rise of large language models has created new opportunities for making MLPS work more intelligent. However, in standards-intensive and security-sensitive scenarios, general-purpose large language models often cannot ensure controllable reasoning or complete understanding of rules. This paper proposes a large language model framework for MLPS that integrates multiple retrieval strategies. It combines hierarchical retrieval, tree-based retrieval, and tokenization-based matching retrieval. This design helps maintain retrieval coverage while reducing the interference of irrelevant context in the reasoning process. To address the requirements of MLPS question answering for clause accuracy, conclusion traceability, and practical deployability, this paper adopts a evaluation method based on multi-dimensional weighted scoring to quantitatively assess model responses. In comparative experiments on ten typical questions, the proposed domain-specific large language model for MLPS achieved higher overall scores.

</details>


### [190] [There Will Be Spam: Characterizing State-Invariant Transactions and Speculative MEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24172)
*Vabuk Pahari,Johnnatan Messias,Christof Ferreira Torres*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模测量了以太坊、Optimism 和 Base 上的状态不变交易（约14亿笔），发现其在Layer-2链上占比高达24%-37%，主要源于投机性MEV提取和地址投毒等恶意活动，减少这类交易可显著降低区块链资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链的透明性和不可篡改性导致账本持续膨胀，威胁去中心化。作者希望识别并量化那些不影响最终状态的无用交易（状态不变交易），以揭示资源浪费的严重性。

Method: 定义状态不变交易（包含或移除后除手续费外不影响账户状态），然后在以太坊、Optimism 和 Base 三条链上对交易进行大规模测量和分类分析。

Result: 识别出近14亿笔状态不变交易。以太坊占2.6%，Optimism占24%，Base占37%。投机性MEV是Layer-2上的主要来源（57%和68%），但并非唯一来源；以太坊上地址投毒占非回滚状态不变交易的53%。

Conclusion: 减少状态不变交易可大幅降低区块链资源消耗和交易成本，同时有助于限制钓鱼攻击等恶意行为。

Abstract: Blockchains rely on transparency and immutability to ensure trust, but these guarantees come at the cost of an ever-growing ledger that increasingly threatens decentralization by making it more expensive to store and maintain the full transaction history. In this work, we introduce state-invariant transactions, defined as transactions whose inclusion or removal does not affect the resulting blockchain state beyond transaction fees. We argue that these transactions constitute a form of on-chain spam because they consume execution, bandwidth, storage, and blockspace without contributing to the final ledger state. We present the first large-scale measurement of state-invariant transactions across Ethereum, Optimism, and Base, identifying nearly 1.4 billion such transactions. While only 2.6% of Ethereum transactions are state-invariant, they account for 24% of transactions on Optimism and 37% on Base, representing a significant source of unnecessary resource consumption on Layer-2 blockchains. We show that speculative Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) is the dominant source of state-invariant transactions on Optimism and Base, accounting for 57% and 68%, respectively, but is not the only source as previously assumed. Moreover, despite its popularity, speculative MEV is not the most profitable strategy once the costs of state-invariant transactions are considered. Beyond MEV, we identify substantial malicious activity, with address poisoning campaigns accounting for 53% of non-reverted state-invariant transactions on Ethereum. Our findings suggest that mitigating state-invariant transactions could substantially reduce blockchain resource consumption and transaction costs while limiting phishing campaigns and other forms of blockchain abuse.

</details>


### [191] [Just Testing, Move Along: Evasion of LLM-based System Log Interpretation by Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24174)
*Max Landauer,Florian Skopik,Markus Wurzenberger,Franciszek Górski,Mateusz Krzysztoń*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对基于大语言模型(LLM)的日志分析系统的提示注入攻击，并提出了一套评估框架。实验表明，攻击者可通过在日志中注入恶意内容使LLM将恶意日志误判为良性，同时发现LLM生成的解释中常包含可被用于检测此类攻击的对抗性操作迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用于安全运营中心的日志分析，但其对对抗性日志注入的鲁棒性尚缺乏系统研究，存在难以被察觉的提示注入攻击风险。

Method: 利用真实网络攻击日志，通过通用注入生成、精炼和攻击特定优化三个阶段创建对抗样本，并在多个主流LLM上进行评估。

Result: 对抗性注入能够成功使LLM将含有明显入侵指标的恶意日志误判为良性；但LLM生成的分类解释中经常包含可被用来检测这种对抗性操作的迹象。

Conclusion: LLM日志分析系统面临提示注入攻击的严重威胁，但模型自身的解释性输出可作为一种潜在的防御机制来检测此类攻击。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into Security Operations Center (SOC) workflows, where they support analysts in tasks such as the interpretation of system logs. However, the ability of LLMs to directly process untrusted textual input also introduces new attack surfaces. In particular, attackers can inject contextual information or explicit instructions into log entries in order to influence how malicious activity is interpreted by the model. Despite the growing adoption of LLMs for log analytics, the robustness of such systems against adversarial log injection remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper presents a framework for evaluating prompt injection attacks against LLM-based log interpretation. Using log traces generated during real cyber attacks, our approach creates adversarial examples through generic injection generation, refinement, and attack-specific optimization. Our evaluation across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs shows that these injections can cause malicious log traces to be classified as benign despite containing clear indicators of compromise. As a potential remedy, we show that the explanations generated by the LLMs alongside their classifications frequently contain indicators of adversarial manipulation that can be leveraged to detect such attacks.

</details>


### [192] [EXE-Bench: Ranking the Tradeoffs of AI-based Windows Malware Detectors for Real-World Usability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24177)
*Andrea Ponte,Daniel Gibert,Matous Kozak,Dmitrijs Trizna,Maura Pintor,Battista Biggio,Fabio Roli,Luca Demetrio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EXE-Bench基准，用于系统评估基于AI的Windows恶意软件检测器，涵盖性能、时间鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和计算开销，发现传统特征工程方法在长期性能和对抗防御方面仍优于多数深度网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI恶意软件检测器缺乏系统性评估，存在使用不同数据、未考虑时间变化、忽视对抗攻击和计算开销等问题，无法确定最佳的部署方案。

Method: 开发了EXE-Bench综合基准，将性能、时间鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和计算开销聚合为单一评分，实现公平比较。

Result: 部署后评估无法提供完整性能画像；传统特征工程方法在时间维度和对抗攻击下表现稳健，而多数深度网络仅在部署初期表现优异。

Conclusion: 领域知识驱动的特征工程在恶意软件检测领域仍极具价值，深度网络虽短期表现好但长期鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: Due to the lack of systematic evaluations, we are not yet able to determine which AI-based Windows malware detector to deploy in production, since existing evaluations (i) differ in terms of data used for both training and testing; (ii) do not consider temporal analysis to showcase whether models withstand the passage of time; (iii) avoid security evaluations with adversarial attacks that could highlight their brittleness against content-injection attacks; and (iv) neglect the computational requirements for deployment, risking slow inference on endpoints. For these reasons, we develop EXE-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of AI-based Windows malware detectors. EXE-Bench assesses performance, temporal and adversarial robustness, and computational overhead, aggregating them into a single score for direct and fair model comparison. Through EXE-Bench, we highlight how evaluations conducted only after deployment are suboptimal and unable to provide a complete picture of their performance. In particular, through our analysis, we remark how much domain knowledge instilled through feature engineering is still extremely useful in this domain, resisting both time and adversarial attacks, in stark contrast with most of the deep networks that only excel right after deployment.

</details>


### [193] [DeepFaith: Evidence-Grounded LLMs for Faithful Incident Reporting in Multi-Stage APT Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24348)
*Trung V. Phan,Tri Gia Nguyen,Thomas Bauschert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DeepFaith是一个基于证据的多阶段APT攻击事件报告框架，通过统一证据表示、证据引导提示、忠实感知生成和生成后验证，将自主防御系统的结构化输出转化为忠实于系统证据的自然语言报告。实验表明，该方法将忠实度从0.68提升至0.92，未支持声明从0.32降至0.08，时间一致性从0.6提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的APT自主防御系统输出难以被分析师理解，而大语言模型在生成报告时存在幻觉和缺乏证据支撑的问题，亟需一种能生成忠实于系统证据的可解释报告的方法。

Method: DeepFaith框架包含四个核心模块：统一证据表示、证据引导提示、忠实感知生成和后生成验证，确保证据与生成的报告内容对齐。

Result: 在真实企业测试环境中，忠实度从0.68提升至0.92，未支持声明比例从0.32降至0.08，时间一致性从0.6提升至0.88，同时保持了简洁报告和较低的错误率。

Conclusion: 证据引导的生成方法能够实现可靠、可解释且可操作的APT事件报告，对安全运营中心具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) are difficult to detect and interpret due to their multi-stage and stealthy nature. While recent autonomous defense systems leverage provenance graphs and learning-based models for detection and mitigation, their outputs remain largely machine-oriented and difficult for analysts to interpret. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising interface for report generation, but often produce hallucinated or weakly grounded content. In this paper, we propose DeepFaith, an evidence-grounded framework for faithful incident reporting in multi-stage APT defense. DeepFaith transforms structured outputs from autonomous defense and explainability modules into natural-language reports that are explicitly aligned with underlying system evidence. The framework integrates a unified evidence representation, evidence-grounded prompting, faithfulness-aware generation, and post-generation verification to ensure that all generated statements are supported. Experiments in a realistic enterprise testbed demonstrate that DeepFaith improves faithfulness from 0.68 to 0.92, reduces unsupported claims from 0.32 to 0.08, and increases temporal consistency from 0.6 to 0.88, while maintaining concise reports and lower error rates than existing template-based and LLM-based solutions. These results show that evidence-grounded generation enables reliable, interpretable, and actionable reporting for security operations centers.

</details>


### [194] [When LLM Defenses Backfire: Characterizing Safety, Performance, and Cost Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24392)
*Tong Zhang,Zexin Li,Simin Chen,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM越狱防御策略的副作用，发现防御措施在提升安全性的同时会损害模型性能、增加过度拒绝和推理成本，并给出不同防御策略的权衡指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注防御的安全有效性，忽略了防御措施引入的二次成本（性能下降、过度拒绝、推理开销），缺乏对防御策略副作用维度的系统分析。

Method: 按操作策略对防御方法分类，从性能影响、良性输入的过度拒绝、推理成本三个维度系统评估防御策略的副作用，在多个SOTA防御方法、基准数据集和开源LLM上进行实验。

Result: 防御措施很少提升下游能力；规则型防御能最好地保持任务性能；高度保守的自我反思型防御容易增加过度拒绝；多轮防御的运行时开销最大。

Conclusion: 防御策略在安全性增益与可用性、效率之间存在不同模式的权衡，研究为评估防御副作用提供了基准，并为部署约束下选择防御策略提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Jailbreak defenses are essential for protecting large language models (LLMs), but they can also introduce secondary costs that weaken model utility. We present a systematic study of these defense trade-offs along three dimensions: performance impact, over-refusal on benign inputs, and inference cost. Rather than treating defenses as a single class, we organize them by operational strategy and examine how different strategies correlate with different side-effect profiles. Across state-of-the-art defense methods, widely used benchmark datasets, and representative open-source LLMs, we find that defenses rarely improve downstream capability, but instead vary in how they trade safety gains against usability and efficiency. In particular, rule-based defenses best preserve task performance, highly conservative self-reflective defenses often increase over-refusal, and multi-round defenses incur the largest runtime overhead. These results provide both a benchmark for evaluating defense side effects and practical guidance for selecting defenses under deployment constraints.

</details>


### [195] [Occluded Oculus: Operationalizing Stylistic Obscurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24411)
*Robert Dilworth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过对抗性策略（TraceTarnish）匿名化文本作者身份的有效性，发现其中"注入"模块（插入零宽Unicode字符、同形异义词和故意拼写错误）最能有效对抗文体计量系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战者需要克服文体计量系统和监控设备这一"多眼巨人"，以找回被窃取的隐私。

Method: 对TraceTarnish系统进行消融研究，比较其四个模块：翻译、混淆、模仿和注入。

Result: 注入模块（插入零宽Unicode字符、同形异义词和故意拼写错误）是最有效的匿名化方法。

Conclusion: 巧妙的对抗策略对于战胜强大的文体计量系统是必要的，其中注入式攻击最为有效。

Abstract: What did it take for Hermes, the devout messenger of the Olympian gods, to slay Argus Panoptes, the multi-eyed giant of Greek myth? As the perfect guardian, Panoptes' legion of ever-watchful eyes proved difficult -- but not impossible -- to defeat. The centerpiece of Hermes' strategy was obfuscation and sabotage. Posing as a shepherd, Hermes sealed each of Panoptes' eyes -- eyes that would otherwise have alerted the fearsome giant to Hermes' plot -- and vanquished him. The moral of the story: when a challenger must surmount a formidable foe -- one far greater in stature and vastly more equipped -- crafty maneuvers are not merely advisable but indispensable for victory. In this work, the "challenger" is a collective leveraging adversarial tactics to overcome the "multi-eyed giant" of stylometric systems and surveillance apparatuses. To successfully claw back the privacy siphoned by the multi-eyed giant, the challenger must carefully evaluate their plan of attack, $\textit{TraceTarnish}$, and determine what does and does not work to anonymize the authorship of text. To that end, we conduct an ablation study of $\textit{TraceTarnish}$ to better understand which module -- Translation, Obfuscation, Imitation, or Injection -- best confounds a stylometric system. Our results indicate that the most effective approach was Injection, meaning that inserting zero-width Unicode characters, homoglyphs, and intentional misspellings neutralizes the indefatigable eyes long enough to claim the head of the all-seeing giant.

</details>


### [196] [Near-Tight Theoretical Bounds for Incentive Compatibility in Bitcoin Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24415)
*Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对工作量证明区块链的激励兼容性阈值问题，提出了更现实的模型和算法，将上下界误差缩小到9.98×10⁻⁴以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Sapirshtein等人的MDP方法和Kiayias等人的区块链挖矿博弈模型）存在两个局限：模型限制挖矿者行动空间过窄、假设理想化的平局行为，且理论上下界不够紧致。本文旨在解决这两大局限。

Method: 构建更现实的模型：扩展挖矿者行动空间，引入非对称平局打破参数γ⁻和γ⁺；设计算法计算激励兼容性阈值的上下界。

Result: 所提算法能将上下界误差控制在9.98006×10⁻⁴以内，显著优于此前方法的紧致性。

Conclusion: 通过放宽模型假设并设计精确算法，本文有效提升了激励兼容性阈值估计的精度，为PoW区块链的理性挖矿激励设计提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: When is honest Bitcoin mining rational? This question is central to the incentive design of proof-of-work blockchains. Sapirshtein et al. computationally derived near-tight lower and upper bounds on the incentive-compatibility threshold using a Markov Decision Process. Kiayias et al.'s Blockchain Mining Games instead derived theoretical lower and upper bounds. However, this theoretical approach has two limitations: its model restricts miners to a narrow action space and assumes idealized tie behavior, and its lower and upper bounds are far from tight.
  We resolve both limitations. We develop a more realistic model with a broader miner action space and asymmetric tie-breaking parameters $γ^-$ and $γ^+$. We then propose an algorithm that computes lower and upper bounds on the incentive-compatibility threshold with a maximum error of $9.98006\times10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [197] [VulnGym: Evaluating Vulnerability Management Strategies against Advanced Persistent Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24552)
*Sofia Della Penna,Lorenzo Parracino,Luciano Pianese,Vittorio Orbinato,Roberto Natella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VulnGym是一个模拟工具，用于评估漏洞管理策略，通过RL训练的对抗真实APT的攻击者来测试不同补丁策略的效果。实验表明漏洞管理需结合组织环境、攻击行为、网络拓扑和资产重要性进行定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞优先级评分标准（如CVSS）只能单独评估漏洞，无法反映补丁策略在对抗随时间在网络中推进的APT攻击者时的整体表现；已有RL模拟工具要么忽略漏洞管理，要么使用合成网络与真实威胁数据脱节。

Method: VulnGym构建了RL训练的攻击者（基于真实APT档案校准）与执行可配置补丁策略的防御者之间的对抗模拟环境，双方在共享的动态网络表示上行动，攻击者进展直接受防御者补丁活动影响。

Result: 基于真实漏洞和两个APT组织的实验表明，漏洞管理需要针对组织环境、攻击行为、网络拓扑和资产重要性进行定制化设计。

Conclusion: VulnGym填补了现有工具在评估补丁策略对抗真实APT攻击者方面的空白，为资源受限的组织提供了更贴近实际的漏洞管理策略评估手段。

Abstract: Enterprise networks are continuously targeted by Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs), attack campaigns exploiting software vulnerabilities to compromise critical assets over time. As disclosed vulnerabilities grow, resource-constrained organizations must prioritize which ones to patch. Existing prioritization standards score vulnerabilities individually and cannot capture how a patching policy performs against an adversary that progresses through the network over time. Previous tools have simulated attack campaigns through Reinforcement Learning (RL), but either omit vulnerability management, leaving the attacker unopposed, or rely on synthetic networks disconnected from real threat data, and so cannot assess how a policy would fare against a realistic adversary. To fill this gap, we propose VulnGym, a simulation tool to evaluate vulnerability management policies. VulnGym simulates an RL-trained attacker, calibrated on real APT profiles, against a defender executing a configurable patching policy over a network with real Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVEs). Both agents act on a shared, evolving network representation, so the attacker's progress is directly shaped by the defender's patching activity, allowing a given policy to be stress-tested against a realistic attack campaign. Experiments based on real-world vulnerabilities and two APTs show that vulnerability management must be tailored to organizational context, adversarial behavior, network topology, and asset criticality.

</details>


### [198] [Modeling Local Exploit Hazard - A Bayesian Framework for Quantifying Exploit Risk and Operational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24618)
*Stephen Shaffer,Laura Voicu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯框架，将全局性漏洞利用概率（如EPSS）转换为组织内部资产的每日利用风险率，并结合防御措施有效性评估与生存分析，支持多层级风险聚合与修复优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞利用预测模型（如EPSS）提供的是全局性概率，无法直接反映特定组织的实际防御环境，导致安全团队难以基于自身情况进行精准的漏洞修复优先级排序。

Method: 构建贝叶斯框架，先通过专家意见池和Beta-二项推断生成防御措施有效性的概率分布，再通过攻击向量对齐修正EPSS分数；利用生存分析（指数风险/威布尔风险）将漏洞利用可能性转为风险率，并支持按主机、网络、业务单元、组织层级求和聚合。

Result: 实现了从全局漏洞评分到组织特定风险率的转换，支持对候选修复方案进行量化模拟与排序，为安全团队在固定资源下提供可辩护的优先级决策依据。

Conclusion: 该模型为组织提供了将通用漏洞评分本地化的定量方法，未来可扩展至事件可能性与财务损失建模。

Abstract: This paper presents a local exploit hazard model : a Bayesian framework that converts the global probabilities produced by an exploit likelihood model (ELM), such as the Exploit Prediction Scoring System (EPSS), into a daily exploit hazard rate for an organization's own assets. The model measures the exploit-prevention effectiveness of deployed controls as a probability distribution. That distribution is seeded from a subject-matter-expert opinion pool and updated through Beta-Binomial inference from telemetry, breach-and-attack simulation, or penetration testing, then applied to ELM scores by attack-vector alignment. The resulting per-vulnerability exploitation likelihoods are converted into hazard rates using standard survival-analysis techniques, supporting both a constant exponential hazard and a Weibull hazard whose shape parameter, calibrated from Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalog timing, captures the empirical decay of exploitation risk as a vulnerability ages. Because hazards are additive under independence, per-vulnerability rates aggregate by summation up to host, network, business unit, and organization. Candidate remediation actions are simulated and ranked by projected hazard reduction, giving defenders a defensible, quantitative basis for prioritization under fixed capacity. Future work includes extensions for incident likelihood and financial loss modeling.

</details>


### [199] [Agentic Permissions Policy Algebra for Taint Confinement in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24625)
*Arseny Kravchenko,Vadim Liventsev,Innokentii Konstantinov,Ildar Iskhakov,Matvey Kukuy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APPA是一个通过引擎管理的上下文分支和前瞻性获取强制来解决动态信息流控制中可用性瓶颈的框架，在抑制数据泄露的同时恢复被污染追踪牺牲的实用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态IFC的污点追踪在读取未经验证数据时会永久污染代理上下文，严重限制下游实用性，需要一种既能保证安全又不过度牺牲可用性的方法。

Method: 提出APPA框架，核心包括：(1) 前瞻性标签评估和补救计划生成；(2) 标签种子化的子轨迹分支，允许可信清洗器返回有界导数至未改变的父上下文；(3) 基于双幺半群模型的安全标签和共享事件日志，形式化证明了父标签保持和合并隔离。

Result: 在四模型多轮工具链基准测试中，APPA将数据泄露成功率从31%-50%降至0%-7%；在三个模型上，分支机制恢复了污点追踪损失的实质性实用性能。

Conclusion: APPA通过上下文分支和前瞻性获取强制有效解决了IFC的安全性与可用性权衡问题，为自主LLM代理处理混合机密性数据提供了结构性安全保障。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents processing mixed-confidentiality data face severe security risks from prompt injection attacks and reasoning errors. While dynamic Information Flow Control (IFC) provides structural security guarantees, traditional taint tracking permanently taints an agent's context upon reading unvetted data, severely restricting downstream utility. We present APPA (Agentic Permissions Policy Algebra), an IFC framework that resolves this usability bottleneck through engine-managed context branching and prospective acquisition enforcement. Before data acquisition occurs, APPA prospectively evaluates label descents and missing prerequisites, generating actionable remedy plans (Authorize, Accept). To inspect unvetted data without polluting the primary context, a label-seeded child trajectory is spawned, absorbing label descent locally and allowing a trusted sanitizer to return a bounded derivative to the unchanged parent. Governed by a two-monoid model over security labels and shared event logs, we formally prove parent label preservation and merge confinement. Finally, we evaluate APPA on a multi-turn tool-chaining benchmark across four models: it suppresses exfiltration (31%-50% down to 0%-7% attack success), and on three of the four, branching recovers a substantial share of the utility that taint tracking alone forfeits.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [StateAct: Program State, before Pixels, for Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22798)
*Yan Yang,Xiangru Jian,Ziyang Luo,Zirui Zhao,Yutong Dai,Ziji Shi,Hanshu Yan,Jun Hao Liew,Silvio Savarese,Junnan Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StateAct 提出了一种以代码优先、状态驱动的多智能体框架，让主智能体直接操作程序状态（而非截图），仅在极少数子任务中使用GUI子代理处理截图-点击交互。该方法显著提升了任务成功率，同时大幅降低了成本，并指出瓶颈从感知转向了推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机使用智能体仅依赖截图（有损渲染）来理解程序状态，但截图丢失了大量底层状态信息（如文件、DOM、后端数据），不同状态可能产生相同像素，这限制了智能体的感知和决策能力。作者希望绕过这一瓶颈，让智能体直接访问和操作程序状态。

Method: 提出StateAct多智能体框架：主智能体通过代码直接操作程序状态；专用GUI子智能体仅在少数需要截图-点击交互的子目标中介入（108个任务中仅28个用到，占主智能体步骤的1.1%）；独立的finish gate对保存结果进行结构验证；主智能体将子目标委托给新的子智能体，保持自身上下文聚焦。

Result: 在OSWorld 2.0上，StateAct将Claude Opus 4.8的二元成功率从20.6%提升至26.9%，部分成功率从54.8%提升至61.6%，且每任务成本降低约9倍。纯代码变体（无GUI子代理）的部分成功率仅为45.9%，低于基于截图的基线54.8%。

Conclusion: 将行动、验证和记忆都基于程序状态（即状态接地），将主要瓶颈从感知转移到推理——失败更多取决于智能体"想什么"而非"看到什么"。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are usually improved by strengthening perception: better models for reading a screenshot and choosing where to click. Yet a screenshot is only a lossy rendering of the underlying program state, e.g., the files, application backends, and DOM that hold the task data. Different states can produce the same pixels, while code can inspect and modify that state directly. StateAct is a code-first, multi-agent harness built around this distinction. Its main agent works directly with program state by using code, while a dedicated GUI subagent handles screenshot-and-click interaction on the few subgoals that need it, just 28 of 108 tasks and 1.1% of main-agent steps. The same direct access to program state also supports verification: an independent finish gate double-checks the saved result for structural failures, e.g., output that is missing, unsaved, or written to the wrong path. To stay on track over hundreds of steps, the main agent hands subgoals to fresh subagents, keeping its own context focused. On OSWorld 2.0, StateAct lifts Claude Opus 4.8 from 20.6% to 26.9% on binary success, and from 54.8% to 61.6% on partial success, at ~ 9x lower cost per task than the same model driven by screenshots alone; a code-only variant with no GUI subagent reaches only 45.9% partial, below that screenshot-based baseline's 54.8%. In general, grounding action, verification, and memory in state, what we call state-grounding, shifts the main bottleneck from perception toward reasoning: failures depend more on what the agent thinks than on what it sees.

</details>


### [201] [The Best Programming Language for Tokenmaxxing: An Investigation of Coding Agent Behavior Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22807)
*Zixuan Wu,Carolyn Jane Anderson,Arjun Guha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示编程语言会显著影响编码智能体的token消耗，跨模型具有一致性。通过分析智能体轨迹的结构和内容，发现语言不熟悉会导致反复产生不可编译的解决方案和额外工作，以及代理在代码注释中规划、自创测试输入等行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体在多种编程语言中表现出色，但不同语言间的token成本差异尚未被系统研究。

Method: 选取Python、Java、Rust、OCaml四种语言，使用五个最新模型进行编程问题测试，控制问题难度。通过重新执行中间方案并抽象为测试结果向量序列来结构化分析轨迹，同时分析轨迹文本内容。

Result: 不同语言间token消耗存在显著且跨模型一致的差异；智能体在不熟悉语言中反复产生不可编译的解决方案并修改已通过的方案；智能体在代码注释中规划、不信任提供的测试而自创输入、通过Python原型绕过不熟悉的目标语言。

Conclusion: 按语言区分的token效率应作为多语言智能体基准测试和发展的考量指标，同时为追求token消耗最大化的用户提供语言选择指南。

Abstract: Although coding agents are now very effective in a variety of programming languages, this paper first shows that the cost (in tokens) can very significantly by programming language. We evaluate five recent models on programming problems in Python, Java, Rust, and OCaml. We carefully control for problem difficulty, and show that there can be stark variation in token consumption that is consistent across models. To understand why, we analyze both the structure and content of agent trajectories. First, we re-execute every intermediate solution and abstract each trajectory as a sequence of test-outcome vectors, then label the work between successive solutions. This reveals agents repeatedly producing noncompiling solutions in unfamiliar languages and revising solutions that already pass. Second, we analyze trajectory text, finding that agents plan solutions in code comments, distrust the provided tests in favor of inputs they invent, and sidestep unfamiliar target languages by prototyping in Python.
  Our results show that by-language token efficiency is a metric that should be considered when benchmarking and developing multilingual agents, and, for the tokenmaxxer, a guide to the most expensive language to work in.

</details>


### [202] [Do Coverage and Mutation Scores of LLM-Generated Test Suites Correlate with Their Effectiveness? (Replicability Study)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22880)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模复制研究，发现LLM生成的测试用例中，代码覆盖率和变异分数与真实bug检测能力的关系具有高度上下文依赖性，在回归测试场景下可作为有效指标，但在检测已有bug时不可靠；同时测试套件大小并非主要混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Inozemtseva等人和Papadakis等人）发现人类编写的测试中，覆盖率、变异分数与真实bug检测的相关性在控制测试套件大小后会消失。但LLM生成的测试与人类编写的测试工作流差异很大，这些结论是否适用于LLM生成的测试尚不明确，因此需要进行复制研究验证。

Method: 使用多种LLM生成的大范围测试套件，对两项先前工作进行了大规模复制研究，重新检验了覆盖率、变异分数和真实bug检测效果之间的关系。

Result: 发现覆盖率与变异分数的有效性高度依赖上下文：在回归测试场景（代码可合理假设无bug）中，这些指标能在模型间比较时提供有意义的信号；但在被测代码可能已有bug的场景中，这些指标不再是可靠的指示器。同时，测试套件大小并非主要混淆因素。

Conclusion: 为评估LLM-based测试生成提供了可操作指南，并帮助解释先前研究的结果。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven growing interest in using LLMs to automate test generation. Prior work commonly evaluates generated test suites using proxy metrics such as code coverage and mutation score. However, studies by Inozemtseva et al. and Papadakis et al. show that, for human-written tests, correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection can largely vanish once test suite size is controlled, raising concerns about the validity of evaluations based on proxy metrics. It also remains unclear whether these conclusions carry over to LLM-generated tests, given that prevailing LLM-based test-generation workflows differ substantially from traditional approaches.
  In this paper, we conduct a large-scale replication study of these two prior works using a wide range of test suites generated by a diverse set of LLMs, and re-examine the relationships among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection effectiveness. Our findings diverge substantially from prior results. We show that the usefulness of coverage and mutation is highly context-dependent: in regression-style settings where the code provided to the LLM can be reasonably assumed bug-free, these metrics can provide meaningful signals when comparing across models; in another common scenario where the code-under-test may already be buggy and the goal is to expose the bug within the code-under-test, they no longer serve as reliable indicators. We also find little evidence that test suite size is a dominant confounder for correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection for LLM-generated tests. Based on these findings, we discuss how to interpret results from prior studies and provide actionable guidance for evaluating LLM-based test generation.

</details>


### [203] [Evaluating and Mitigating the Misguidance Effect of Buggy Code in LLM-Generated Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22883)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现用有缺陷的代码提示LLM生成单元测试会产生"误导效应"——模型倾向于生成验证错误行为而非发现缺陷的测试。为此提出基于规格说明的测试生成范式，用LLM生成的规格文档替代待测代码，有效减少误导测试并增加有效测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明用有缺陷代码提示LLM会降低生成测试的质量，但缺乏对这一现象的量化度量方法，也缺少有效的缓解策略。

Method: 提出一种量化"误导效应"的指标，从模型内部视角分析有缺陷代码如何扭曲LLM的测试偏好；引入基于规格说明的测试生成范式，用LLM生成的规格文档替换prompt中的待测代码。

Result: 有缺陷代码会显著增加断言错误行为的误导测试，同时抑制有效缺陷发现测试；基于规格说明的范式能有效减少误导测试、增加有效测试，并提升多轮反馈驱动的测试生成流程。

Conclusion: 基于规格说明的提示策略是缓解LLM生成单元测试时受有缺陷代码误导的有效方法，且同时适用于有缺陷和无缺陷代码场景。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for automating unit test generation, recent studies suggest that the quality of generated tests can be negatively impacted when models are prompted with buggy code. This paper presents a new metric to quantitatively measure the "misguidance effect," a phenomenon where buggy code steers LLMs toward generating tests that validate its erroneous behavior rather than expose it. Our analysis reveals that prompting LLMs with buggy code has a severe, twofold impact: it significantly increases "misguided tests" that assert incorrect behavior while simultaneously suppressing the generation of effective, bug-finding tests. We further corroborate this effect from a model-internal perspective, showing that buggy code skews LLMs' preference toward tests that assert the same erroneous behavior. To counter this, we introduce and validate a specification-based unit test generation paradigm that replaces the code under test in the prompt with an LLM-generated specification docstring. Our results show that this paradigm effectively reduces misguided tests while substantially increasing effective tests, improves multi-round, feedback-driven test generation pipelines, and remains applicable to both buggy and bug-free code. Overall, these results suggest that specification-based prompting is a promising strategy for mitigating misguidance from buggy code in LLM-generated unit tests.

</details>


### [204] [AssumptionMiner: Extracting, Tracing, and Revising Implicit Assumptions in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22898)
*Jie "JW" Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AssumptionMiner框架，将LLM代码生成中的隐式假设显式化，通过结构化假设层、AST依赖图和目标重生成机制，提升代码生成的透明度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从自然语言提示生成代码时，现实中的提示往往缺少完整规范，LLM会填充隐式假设，这些假设未被显式检查，可能导致生成的代码满足测试但违背开发者意图。

Method: 提出AssumptionMiner框架：(1) 生成显式假设层，结构化表示推断的约束和设计决策；(2) 构建AST依赖图，支持针对修改的假设进行目标重生成；(3) 构建包含180个模糊编程任务和676个标注假设的基准测试集；(4) 采用置信度加权集成方法进行假设提取。

Result: 在开源LLM上，置信度加权集成在假设提取中F1达0.816，比最强离线基线提升3.6倍；AST引导定位在人工验证基准上比关键词和全文件基线更精确；目标重生成比非目标方案修改更少代码。

Conclusion: 将隐式假设显式化能够提升LLM代码生成的透明度和可控性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate code from natural-language prompts, yet real-world prompts rarely provide complete specifications. When prompts leave input formats, error handling, or design decisions unspecified, LLMs fill these gaps with implicit assumptions that shape the generated code's behavior and correctness. Because these assumptions remain hidden, generated code may satisfy tests while violating developer intent. We present AssumptionMiner, a framework that makes implicit assumptions a first-class artifact of LLM-based code generation. In addition to code, AssumptionMiner produces an explicit assumption layer, a structured representation of inferred constraints and design decisions that developers can inspect, confirm, or revise. An AST-based dependency graph enables targeted regeneration of only the code affected by a revised assumption. We also introduce a benchmark of 180 ambiguous programming tasks with 676 annotated assumptions, including a human-verified subset for evaluating code localization. We evaluate assumption extraction, code localization, and assumption-guided regeneration. Across open-source LLMs, a confidence-weighted ensemble achieves an F1 score of 0.816 for assumption extraction, improving on the strongest offline baseline by 3.6x. On the human-verified localization benchmark, AST-guided localization identifies more precise code regions than keyword-based and whole-file baselines. During assumption revision, targeted regeneration modifies less code than non-targeted alternatives while exposing challenges in handling cascading edits. These results demonstrate that making assumptions explicit improves the transparency and controllability of LLM-based code generation.

</details>


### [205] [Metamorphic Testing for Clinical ML Models: A Framework Proposal and Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22984)
*Jie JW Wu,Feiyu E,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将蜕变测试（MT）应用于临床机器学习模型，以评估其行为正确性，而不仅仅是依赖AUROC等排名指标。通过设计12个基于临床指南的蜕变关系（MRs）并在三个ICU预测任务上验证，发现即使模型AUROC得分很高（0.849-0.900），MT违反率仍高达27%-87%，且AUROC无法检测到的符号反转错误会导致MT违反率上升31-67个百分点。蜕变测试可作为传统性能指标的有效补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型（如住院死亡率和败血症发作预测）虽然AUROC得分很高，但AUROC只衡量排序性能而非临床合理性——模型可能在整体排名上表现良好，却在患者SOFA评分恶化时预测更低死亡风险，这与医学常识相悖。需要一种不依赖真实标签就能评估模型行为正确性的方法。

Method: 1) 设计12个候选蜕变关系（MRs），每个基于权威临床指南，覆盖三个ICU预测任务（MIMIC-III/IV）；2) 提出五层验证策略确保MRs的临床合理性；3) 在UCI心脏病数据集上进行可行性研究；4) 开展注入故障实验（血压特征符号反转）检测AUROC与MT的敏感性差异。

Result: 三个临床模型AUROC为0.849-0.900，但MT违反率高达27%-87%；注入符号反转错误后AUROC未检测到异常，但MT违反率上升31-67个百分点。

Conclusion: 蜕变测试能有效补充AUROC等传统性能指标，用于评估临床预测模型的行为正确性，发现传统指标无法察觉的违背医学常识的错误行为。

Abstract: Machine learning models for clinical prediction tasks, such as in-hospital mortality and sepsis onset, routinely achieve high AUROC scores. However, AUROC measures ranking performance rather than clinical sensibility. A model may rank patients correctly overall while predicting a lower mortality risk when a patient's SOFA score worsens, contradicting established medical knowledge.
  This paper proposes applying metamorphic testing (MT) to clinical machine learning models to evaluate behavioral correctness without requiring ground-truth labels for individual predictions. We design a catalog of 12 candidate metamorphic relations (MRs) for three ICU prediction tasks using the MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, with each MR grounded in an authoritative clinical guideline. We further propose a five-layer validation strategy to ensure that MRs are clinically sound before deployment.
  As a feasibility study, we evaluate the approach on the UCI Heart Disease dataset. Although the three clinical models achieve strong predictive performance (AUROC = 0.849-0.900), they exhibit MT violation rates ranging from 27% to 87% across five pilot MRs. An injected-fault experiment further shows that a sign-negation error in a blood pressure feature remains undetected by AUROC but increases the MT violation rate by 31-67 percentage points. These findings suggest that metamorphic testing provides a valuable complement to conventional performance metrics for assessing the behavioral correctness of clinical prediction models.

</details>


### [206] [Adversarial Test-Hardening for AI-Written Code: An Instrument Autopsy and a Pre-Registered Causal Estimate of the Critic Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23002)
*Jeff Otterson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对抗性测试增强循环，通过机械裁判（变异测试）让模型自动生成测试用例杀死变异体。实验发现早期报告的跨模型效应是输出截断的仪器伪影，揭示了测试框架的不对称性会影响跨模型比较的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型同时生成代码和测试代码，但覆盖率只记录执行了什么，而非验证了什么。需要更可靠的自动测试生成与验证机制。

Method: 对抗性测试增强循环：Tester模型写测试，变异测试识别存活的注入缺陷，Critic模型专门编写杀死这些变异体的测试，所有判定由机械裁判（变异测试结果）决定，无需模型相互评判。

Result: 实验1杀死105个一次生成遗漏的变异体；发现之前报道的跨模型效应(p=9.5e-66)是输出截断的仪器伪影。实验2中，同源Critic平均增量杀死率78%，跨提供商配置显示正向差异但受单一复现主导。

Conclusion: 跨模型比较可能继承运行框架的不对称性；测试框架的细节（如输出截断）会严重扭曲结论，需通过对抗性审查和预注册设计来规避。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write both code and the tests meant to check it; coverage records what ran, not what was verified. We study an adversarial test-hardening loop under a mechanical oracle: a Tester model writes tests, mutation testing names surviving injected defects, and a Critic model writes tests to kill exactly those, with every verdict decided mechanically, so no model judges another's output. In Experiment 1, on five Python subjects (one same-lineage-loop cell could not be scored), the loop killed 105 mutants that one-shot generation missed and lost none, and the cross-lineage-Critic question returned a pre-declared null. The central finding was an autopsy: an earlier analysis reported a cross-lineage effect at p = 9.5e-66 that was an instrument artifact, an output cap silently truncating the verbose model, caught only by adversarial review of the completed analysis. Review then found a further confound, each arm resampling its own initial suite; Experiment 2 removes it. Under a pre-registered frozen-shared-round-0 design (five replicates on each of four subjects, seeds committed in advance), same-lineage Critic rounds killed 78% of the survivors the frozen initial suite left standing (mean incremental kill rate 0.783, 95% cluster-bootstrap interval [0.592, 0.935]), a within-replicate causal estimate; the cross-provider configuration showed a positive pilot difference (rate gap 0.178, 95% interval [0.039, 0.347]; magnitude dominated by a single replicate) at 5.5x lower arm cost. This compares two named model-provider-harness configurations, not an isolated lineage effect: part of the gap is one configuration's receipted operational failures, including truncation recurrences, now detected and scored rather than laundered. Cross-model comparisons can inherit the asymmetries of the harness that runs them. We release both protocols, all receipts, and the analysis code.

</details>


### [207] [Bifrost: Empowering Pretrained Language Model with Fallibility Representation for Log-Based Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23169)
*Minghua He,Tong Jia,Lingzhe Zhang,Chiming Duan,Xinlong Zhao,Leyi Pan,Cheng Wang,Kangjin Wang,Yinghao Yu,Liping Zhang,Yifan Wu,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Bifrost是一种基于自监督对比学习的日志表征学习方法，通过捕捉日志的多层级故障信息，在异常检测、根因定位和故障识别任务上分别比现有预训练语言模型提升9.83%、18.28%和20.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练语言模型的日志故障诊断方法无法全面捕获日志中反映系统故障的多层级结构信息，导致故障诊断效果不佳。

Method: 受站点可靠性工程师日志分析经验启发，基于自监督对比学习设计策略来学习日志的故障表征。

Result: 在三个公开系统和一个工业ML-as-a-Service系统上，Bifrost在异常检测F1、根因定位HR@k和故障识别Macro-F1上分别平均提升9.83%、18.28%和20.88%。

Conclusion: Bifrost能有效学习日志中的故障表征，显著优于现有预训练语言模型方法。

Abstract: Log-based fault diagnosis is crucial for runtime debugging and maintenance. Existing fault diagnosis methods use language models pre-trained on natural language (PLMs) for log representation. However, system faults are reflected in the multi-level structure of system logs. PLMs pre-trained on natural language struggle to comprehensively capture multi-level fault information, failing to meet the requirements of fault diagnosis. We refer to this information as fallibility representations. To address this problem, we propose a novel log representation learning method, Bifrost. It draws inspiration from the log analysis experience of Site Reliability Engineers and meticulously designs strategies based on self-supervised contrastive learning to learn the fallibility representations of logs. Across three public systems and one industrial ML-as-a-Service system, the log representations produced by Bifrost outperform existing PLMs by average margins of 9.83% in F1 for anomaly detection, 18.28% in HR@k for root cause localization, and 20.88% in Macro-F1 for fault identification.

</details>


### [208] [Backend-Aware Graph Learning for Denoising Outcome Distributions in Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23211)
*Ning Ma,Jun Dai,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Q-BRIDGE是一种基于图学习的方法，利用图Transformer架构将含噪量子执行结果转化为去噪分布，在23个IBM量子后端和6种电路族上，以93.97%-94.90%的精度和82.50%-83.51%的召回率检测缺陷引发的测试失败，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ量子后端上的噪声会干扰程序测试的通过/失败判断，现有方法难以有效消除噪声影响，亟需一种能结合电路结构与后端物理特性的噪声缓解方法。

Method: 采用图Transformer架构对经过编译的量子电路进行编码，同时捕获量子门的特性和连接关系以及物理后端的噪声特征；通过基于FiLM的条件层将噪声观测结果与电路编码融合，生成去噪后的概率分布。

Result: 在23个IBM噪声后端和6种电路族上，Q-BRIDGE在噪声缓解任务中大幅超越现有最优基线；在检测bug引发的测试失败时，精度达93.97%-94.90%，召回率达82.50%-83.51%。

Conclusion: 考虑编译后电路的图结构以及特定量子后端的物理特性，是实现更可靠的噪声感知量子程序测试的有效途径。

Abstract: Testing quantum programs on NISQ (Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum) backends is challenging because the noise disturbs outcome distributions and can affect pass/fail decisions. We present Q-BRIDGE, a graph learning-based approach that converts noisy observations into denoised distributions suitable for oracle-based verification. Q-BRIDGE uses a graph transformer architecture to encode a transpiled quantum circuit, capturing the characteristics of its gates and their connectivity; the physical backend information is encoded together with the logical structure of the circuit. An additional conditioning layer, based on FiLM (Feature-Wise Linear Modulation), takes the encoding as input and integrates noisy observations to produce denoised outcomes. We evaluate Q-BRIDGE on 23 IBM noise backends and 6 circuit families representative of practical workloads. In the first setting, we train a separate Q-BRIDGE model for each backend; in the second setting, we train a single general model shared across all backends. Across both settings, Q-BRIDGE outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline in noise mitigation by a large margin. In testing scenarios with noisy executions, Q-BRIDGE achieves 93.97%-94.90% precision and 82.50%-83.51% recall in detecting bug-induced test failures, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline. These results indicate that considering the graph structure of the transpiled circuits and the physical characteristics of specific quantum backends is a practical route to more reliable noise-aware quantum program testing.

</details>


### [209] [Towards LLM-assisted High-Quality Property Generation for Solidity Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23308)
*Muhammad Wahid,Shahzaib Khan,Mashhood Ali,Muhammad Hassan,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型（LLM）为Solidity智能合约自动生成高质量的属性测试用例，结合变异测试评估质量，发现Gemini Pro 1.5配合提示链技术可达25.99%的平均变异得分，接近人类专家的31.75%，在个别合约上甚至持平（74.34% vs 74.83%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约一旦部署即不可篡改，安全漏洞可能长期暴露于被利用的风险中，传统属性测试依赖人工编写，耗时且昂贵，因此需要自动化生成高质量属性的方法。

Method: 利用多种LLM（如Gemini Pro 1.5）并结合不同提示技术（零样本、少样本、提示链）生成Solidity智能合约的属性测试用例，使用变异测试评估生成属性的质量，并与人类专家编写的属性进行对比。

Result: Gemini Pro 1.5配合提示链技术取得最高平均变异得分25.99%，接近人类专家的31.75%。在LibBit合约上，LLM生成的属性达到74.34%，与人类专家的74.83%几乎持平，但在不同合约间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM具备生成高质量智能合约属性的潜力，在个别场景下可达到专家水平，但整体平均性能仍有提升空间，提示链技术是最有效的提示方式。

Abstract: The immutable nature of smart contracts makes it challenging to fix and patch bugs once they are deployed to a blockchain. This implies that security vulnerabilities may be exposed to possible exploitation for a longer period, necessitating comprehensive pre-deployment testing. Property-based testing combined with fuzzing has proven itself as a promising technique for uncovering vulnerabilities. Traditionally, system properties are written by human experts, which is time-consuming and consequently expensive.With the recent advancement in Large Language Models (LLMs) and their ability to 'understand' natural language and code semantics, it may be possible to generate effective properties. This study, leverages state-of-the-art LLMs to generate high-quality properties for Soliditybased smart contracts. We measure the quality of the generated properties using mutation testing. Our results show that LLMs have the potential to generate high-quality properties that are close to those written by human experts. We extensively evaluate LLMs using various prompting techniques (e.g., zero shot, few shot, and prompt chaining). Overall, we find that Gemini Pro 1.5, when combined with prompt chaining, achieves the highest average mutation score of 25.99% among all studied configurations, closely approaching the human written benchmark of 31.75%. However, our per contract analysis reveals notable variance, particularly for the LibBit contract, where Gemini Pro 1.5 under prompt chaining achieves a mutation score of 74.34%, which is on par with human written properties (74.83%). This highlights that while average performance is informative, individual contract level results demonstrate that LLMs can, in some cases, match expert level property generation.

</details>


### [210] [Enhancing Code Understanding for Impact Analysis by Combining Transformers and Program Dependence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23355)
*Yanfu Yan,Nathan Cooper,Kevin Moran,Gabriele Bavota,Denys Poshyvanyk,Steve Rich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Athena的软件影响分析方法，结合程序依赖图与深度表示学习的概念耦合，无需变更历史和执行信息。在包含25个开源项目的大规模基准Alexandria上，最佳配置在mRR、mAP和HIT@10上分别达到60.32%、35.19%和81.48%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IA方法依赖静态、动态或演化耦合指标，存在脆弱、需昂贵执行分析或大量共变历史等问题，且现有基准规模小、提交信息混杂，难以准确评估。

Method: Athena结合软件系统的依赖图信息与基于深度表示学习的概念耦合方法，无需变更历史和执行信息；同时构建了包含25个开源项目的大规模基准Alexandria，利用细粒度的bug修复提交信息。

Result: 最佳配置在Alexandria基准上mRR、mAP和HIT@10分别达60.32%、35.19%和81.48%；相比简单基线，分别提升10.34%、9.55%和11.68%，具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: Athena通过程序依赖图与概念耦合的新颖组合，显著优于基线方法，证明无需历史和执行信息也能有效进行影响分析。

Abstract: Impact analysis (IA) is a critical software maintenance task that identifies the effects of a given set of code changes on a larger software project with the intention of avoiding potential adverse effects. IA is a cognitively challenging task that involves reasoning about the abstract relationships between various code constructs. Given its difficulty, researchers have worked to automate IA with approaches that primarily use coupling metrics as a measure of the "connectedness" of different parts of a software project. Many of these coupling metrics rely on static, dynamic, or evolutionary information and are based on heuristics that tend to be brittle, require expensive execution analysis, or large histories of co-changes to accurately estimate impact sets.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel IA approach, called Athena, that combines a software system's dependence graph information with a conceptual coupling approach that uses advances in deep representation learning for code without the need for change histories and execution information. Previous IA benchmarks are small, containing fewer than ten software projects, and suffer from tangled commits, making it difficult to measure accurate results. Therefore, we constructed a large-scale IA benchmark, called Alexandria, from 25 open-source software projects, that utilizes fine-grained commit information from bug fixes. On this new benchmark, our best-performing approach configuration achieves mRR, mAP, and HIT@10 scores of 60.32%, 35.19%, and 81.48%, respectively. Through various ablations and qualitative analyses, we show that Athena's novel combination of program dependence graphs and conceptual coupling information leads it to outperform a simpler baseline by 10.34%, 9.55%, and 11.68% with statistical significance.

</details>


### [211] [On AI Safety and Security Technical Debt in Engineering AI-Enabled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23365)
*Muhammad Tukur,Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo,Tao Chen,Nour Ali,Anis Zarrad,Rick Kazman,Marco Agus,Rami Bahsoon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了人工智能技术债务（AITDs）的31种类型，构建了包含7大类的根源分类体系，并提出了一个整合框架AITD-MAP，用于可视化、理解和缓解AI系统中的安全与安全技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在医疗、自动驾驶等高风险领域广泛应用，其复杂性和快速演进带来了新型工程负债（AI技术债务），这些债务具有潜伏性和传播性，导致维护困难、可靠性下降和安全风险增加，亟需系统化识别与治理。

Method: 基于AI信任、风险与安全管理（AI TRiSM）原则，对60篇主要研究进行系统综述，识别31种AITD类型并构建7类根源导向分类体系，分析其与18项信任相关问题的映射关系（6项安全危害和12项安全漏洞），并综合34条可操作指南。

Result: 识别出31种AITD类型，形成7类分类体系；映射到18项信任相关问题（6项安全危害和12项安全漏洞）；综合34条缓解指南（8条安全指南和26条安全指南）；提出AITD-MAP集成框架。

Conclusion: AITD-MAP框架将AI技术债务分类、质量与风险影响以及缓解策略统一整合，有助于AI软件工程师可视化安全与安全技术债务，理解其根源并采取有效缓解措施。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, autonomous driving, finance, and education. While these systems offer powerful data-driven and adaptive capabilities, their complexity, rapid evolution, and dependence on dynamic data pipelines introduce new forms of engineering liability collectively referred to as AI Technical Debts (AITDs). AITDs arise from root causes spanning data governance, model implementation, algorithm design, architectural decisions, operational processes, documentation practices, and testing adequacy. Unlike conventional technical debt, many AITDs are latent and propagate across tightly coupled AI pipelines, leading to maintenance challenges, reliability degradation, and heightened safety or security risks. Guided by the principles of AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (AI TRiSM), this study reinterprets technical debt through the interconnected dimensions of trustworthiness, focusing on AI safety and security technical debts. We conduct a systematic review of 60 primary studies and identify 31 distinct types of AITD, which are organized into a root-cause-oriented taxonomy comprising seven classes. The analysis examines how these debts map to 18 trust-related concerns, including 6 safety hazards and 12 security vulnerabilities. To support mitigation, the review synthesizes 34 actionable guidelines (8 safety and 26 security) targeting the prevention, detection, and reduction of AITDs across the AI lifecycle. Building on these findings, we introduce AITD-MAP, an integrated framework that connects the AITD taxonomy, quality and risk impacts, and mitigation strategies into a unified structure for risk-aware AI engineering. The framework aims to assist AI software engineers in making AI safety and security technical debts visible, understanding their root causes, and mitigating their presence.

</details>


### [212] [TLA$^{+}$-Bench: An Execution-Grounded Benchmark and Dataset for Natural-Language to TLA+ Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23425)
*Arslan Bisharat,Eric Spencer,Brian Ortiz,Khushboo Bhadauria,Mujtaba Nazari,Beatriz Santos,Anisa Ramos,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Läufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TLA$^{+}$-Bench基准，通过模型检测器执行来评判TLA$^{+}$规范的正确性，发现评判标准的选择会导致正确率在1.7%到18.7%之间大幅波动，并将此范围定义为"正确性包络线"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TLA$^{+}$生成评测依赖与参考规范的相似度或语法解析成功与否，无法衡量规范的正确性（是否满足所需属性），需要更精确的评测方法。

Method: 构建TLA$^{+}$-Bench数据集，包含403个经过模型检测验证的黄金规范和897个仅解析通过的银级规范；使用TLA$^{+}$模型检测器对整个可达状态空间进行验证；采用四种不同风格的模型生成描述；通过改变评分选择（如接口名称是否提供等）测量正确率变化范围。

Result: 仅改变评分选择，同一组模型输出的正确率从10.0%到1.7%变化六倍；加上接口供给选择，范围扩大至18.7%到1.7%（十一倍）。最强模型默认正确率16%，给定接口名称后26%；开源模型最高仅1%；正确率随难度急剧下降。

Conclusion: 评测标准的选择对正确率影响巨大，应报告"正确性包络线"而非单一数值；所有模型生成有效TLA$^{+}$的频率远高于生成正确TLA$^{+}$的频率。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write TLA$^{+}$ formal specifications from natural-language descriptions, but progress is hard to measure: existing resources grade by resemblance to a reference or by whether the output parses, neither of which shows correctness. We present TLA$^{+}$-Bench, a dataset and benchmark that grades by execution. Every gold specification ships a configuration the TLA$^{+}$ model checker runs over the full reachable state space, deciding exactly whether the specification holds the properties that configuration names. The dataset holds 403 model-checked gold and 897 parse-only silver specifications from 13 public repositories, subsumes prior TLA$^{+}$ generation data, and carries four model-written descriptions in two styles from two providers, with difficulty and category labels. Our main finding is about measurement itself: an exact oracle gives not one correctness number but a range. Varying only the grading choices earlier benchmarks leave unstated, on one fixed set of model outputs, the correct rate moves sixfold, from 10.0\% to 1.7\%; adding the interface-supply choice, where the model is told the configuration's names, widens the range to elevenfold, from 18.7\% to 1.7\%. We call this range the correctness envelope and measure each of its bounds. The findings inside it are stable. Every model writes valid TLA$^{+}$ far more often than correct TLA$^{+}$: the strongest is correct 16\% of the time by default and 26\% when given the interface names, open models at most 1\%, and correctness falls sharply with difficulty.

</details>


### [213] [LinkRank: A Learning-to-Rank Framework for One-to-Many Issue-Commit Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23610)
*Abhishek Kumar,Tuhin Mondal,Partha Pratim Das,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LinkRank框架，将问题-提交链接恢复视为面向问题的排序与迭代选择问题，而非独立的成对分类。该方法在六个开源项目上显著优于现有基线，在已知与未知链接数量两种设置下均取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多假设问题与提交是一对一关系，但实际中许多问题通过多次提交解决，忽略这种一对多关系会导致追踪不完整。

Method: 提出LinkRank学习排序框架，采用迭代的"选取-移除-重归一化"策略：每次选择排序最高的提交，将其从候选池移除，重归一化剩余分数，重复直至达到停止条件。支持已知K和未知K（ABS/REL停止规则）两种设置。

Result: 在六个项目上，LinkRank的已知K F1为74.54%（基线48.39%）；未知K下ABS为68.84%，REL为67.02%，均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 一对多的问题-提交链接恢复更适合作为面向问题的排序与迭代选择问题来处理，而非独立的成对分类。

Abstract: Recovering traceability links between issues and commits is important for software maintenance, debugging, impact analysis, and project understanding. However, most existing approaches assume a one-to-one relationship, where each issue is linked to a single commit. In practice, many issues are resolved through multiple commits, and ignoring this one-to-many nature can lead to incomplete traceability. This paper presents LinkRank, a learning-to-rank framework for recovering one-to-many issue--commit links. Unlike existing methods that mainly judge issue--commit pairs independently, LinkRank considers the set of candidate commits for an issue and identifies the commits that are most likely to contribute to its resolution. To support realistic evaluation, we construct a new dataset from six open-source GitHub repositories. LinkRank follows an iterative pick--remove--renormalize strategy: it selects the highest-ranked commit, removes it from the candidate pool, renormalizes the remaining scores, and repeats the process until the stopping criterion is met. We evaluate LinkRank under two settings: Known-K, where the true number of linked commits is available, and Unknown-K, where the model must infer when to stop selecting commits using ABS and REL stopping rules. Across six projects, LinkRank achieves an average Known-K F1 score of 74.54%, compared with 48.39% for the strongest baseline. In the Unknown-K setting, LinkRank achieves 68.84% F1 with ABS and 67.02% F1 with REL, outperforming the strongest baselines under both automatic stopping rules. Overall, the findings suggest that one-to-many issue--commit traceability is better addressed as an issue-centric ranking and iterative selection problem than as independent pairwise classification.

</details>


### [214] [Where Is the Cost of Third-Party API Routers in Agentic Software Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23624)
*Donghao Fu,Jingxin Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了第三方API路由器在编程代理工作流中的注入攻击风险，提出了四种渐进的干预级别，开发了SIDEL框架进行评估，发现现有客户端防护措施无法有效检测路由器侧的恶意干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方API路由器位于编程代理与LLM提供商之间，占据可信路径，可检查修改每个请求和响应，但缺乏机制验证提供商输出与代理执行的仓库级操作之间的一致性，导致客户端权限机制可能失效，这一控制缺口对软件开发任务的实际影响尚未被实证测量。

Method: 设计了四种渐进的干预级别：响应替换(L1)、响应追加(L2)、LLM润色注入(L3)和LLM润色+分布对齐注入(L4)。开发了SIDEL框架用于轨迹记录、重放、注入和防御评估，构建了包含400个样本的数据集，评估了四种代表性编程代理以及白名单执行控制和LLM审查两种防御措施。

Result: 路由器侧干预实质性改变仓库级操作，且现有客户端防护措施难以检测。无额外缓解措施时，所有评估代理在所有注入级别上的防御成功率为0%。客户端缓解措施和反应式审查虽能提高抵抗力，但无法完全恢复端到端控制。

Conclusion: 第三方API路由器的控制缺口真实存在且影响显著，现有客户端防护机制不足，需要提供商侧提供输出完整性保证。

Abstract: Third-party API routers have become a common layer that unifies access across increasingly diverse LLM providers. In coding-agent workflows, high-autonomy operation is widely adopted because it reduces interaction overhead. As a result, a third-party API router, which sits between the agent and the upstream provider, inevitably occupies the trusted path. It can inspect and modify every request and response, yet no mechanism verifies alignment between the provider's output and the repository-level actions ultimately executed by the agent. Consequently, client-side permission mechanisms may become ineffective in practice. Whether this control gap produces real, hard-to-detect effects on software development tasks remains empirically unmeasured. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study of router-side injection in coding agents, examining four intervention levels of increasing subtlety: Response Substitution (L1), Response Append (L2), LLM-Polished Injection (L3), and LLM-Polished with Distribution Alignment Injection (L4). Moreover, we develop SIDEL, a framework for trace recording, replay, injection, and defense evaluation, with a curated dataset of 400 samples. We evaluate four representative coding agents, and further evaluate whitelist-based execution control and LLM review. Router-side intervention substantially alters repository-level actions and remains difficult for existing client-side safeguards to detect. Without additional mitigations, all evaluated agents achieved a defense success rate of 0 percent across all injection levels. Client-side mitigations and reactive reviews improve resistance but do not fully restore end-to-end control, motivating provider-side output-integrity guarantees. Our code is available at https://github.com/Riyasushin/SIDE.

</details>


### [215] [Multi-level Code Optimization via Mixture of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23665)
*Yun Peng,Jun Wan,Jiakun Liu,Shuzheng Gao,David Lo,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Optimo，一种基于LLM的多层次代码优化方法，通过Mixture-of-Prompts架构识别性能瓶颈并在四个抽象层级进行优化，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编译优化方法难以处理动态语言，而现有LLM方法无法识别合适的优化目标且仅进行单一层次的不全面优化。

Method: 基于Mixture-of-Prompts (MoP)架构，通过差分分析识别时间关键型代码作为性能瓶颈，将其路由到不同的优化策略（类似MoE中的专家模型），在算法、语句、API使用等四个抽象层级进行优化。

Result: 在COFFE和Effibench基准上，Optimo在优化人工编写代码时达到最高57.48%的opt%和3.97x加速，opt%比最优基线高出96.51%；优化LLM生成代码时达到42.42%的opt%和13.51x加速。

Conclusion: Optimo通过多层次、多策略的LLM代码优化方法，有效克服了传统方法和现有LLM方法的局限性，显著提升了动态语言代码的运行效率。

Abstract: Runtime efficiency is a critical factor that impacts both software quality and user satisfaction. There are many approaches proposed for code optimization to improve runtime efficiency. Traditional code optimization methods operate on intermediate representations (IRs) during compilation for static languages. They are effective but struggle to handle dynamic languages that do not require compilation. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been leveraged to directly optimize source code in dynamic languages. However, these methods fail to identify suitable optimization targets and usually conduct incomprehensive single-level optimization.
  To address these challenges, we propose Optimo, a multi-level LLM-based code optimization approach built on a novel Mixture-of-Prompts (MoP) architecture. In the MoP architecture, Optimo identifies time-critical code structures as performance bottlenecks via differential profiling. These structures are then routed to some optimization strategies, akin to expert models in MoE, each tailored to optimize specific code patterns. Unlike traditional approaches that focus only on statement-level optimizations, Optimo operates at four levels of abstraction, ranging from coarse-grained algorithmic improvements to fine-grained optimizations in API usage. We evaluate Optimo on two code efficiency benchmarks, COFFE and Effibench. Our results demonstrate that Optimo achieves an up to 57.48% opt%, i.e., the percentage of optimized programs that are correct and at least 10% faster than the original programs, and an up to 3.97x speedup when optimizing human-written code, and it consistently outperforms the best baseline by up to 96.51% in terms of opt%. Furthermore, Optimo achieves an up to 42.42% opt% and an up to 13.51x speedup when optimizing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [216] [Semantic-Enhanced Automatic Refinement of Architecture Recovery Results Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23774)
*Yiran Zhang,Chengwei Liu,Yuqiang Sun,Zhengzi Xu,Weisong Sun,Wenke Li,Wuxia Jin,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Semref框架，结合大语言模型与依赖分析，自动优化现有架构恢复工具的结果，在9个项目和10个工具上取得显著准确率提升（归一化增益17.72%-43.35%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构设计与实现之间存在偏差，现有自动架构恢复技术准确率有限，架构师仍需投入大量精力手动修正恢复结果。

Method: 将大语言模型（LLM）的语义理解能力与结构依赖分析相结合，自动优化已有架构恢复工具的输出结果。

Result: 在9个有真实架构标注的项目和10个主流架构恢复工具上测试，各项指标准确率均提升，归一化增益17.72%-43.35%，MoJoFM和a2a_adj指标相对提升分别达118.57%和100.41%。

Conclusion: Semref能够显著减少架构师手动修正恢复结果的精力，有效提高架构恢复的准确性与可理解性。

Abstract: Understanding the architecture is crucial for effectively maintaining and managing large software systems. However, discrepancies often exist between the designed and implemented architectures, which can pose significant risks. To identify these discrepancies, architects need to extract the architecture from the system implementation, which is both time-consuming and error-prone. To simplify this procedure, many automatic architecture recovery techniques have been developed. Yet, their accuracy is often limited. Architects must still invest significant effort in refining recovery results to ensure they accurately reflect the implemented architecture.
  To reduce such manual effort, we introduce Semref, a framework that combines LLMs with dependency analysis to automatically refine architectures recovered by existing architecture recovery tools. By leveraging the LLM's semantic understanding capabilities and integrating structural dependencies, Semref enhances both the accuracy and the comprehension of recovered architectures. To evaluate Semref, we tested on 9 projects with published ground-truth architectures and 10 state-of-the-art architecture recovery tools. 5 commonly used metrics are adopted to evaluate the effectiveness of Semref. The results show that Semref improves accuracy across various metrics, with normalized gains ranges from 17.72\% to 43.35\%. Specifically, for MoJoFM and $a2a_{adj}$ metrics, Semref achieves relative improvements of 118.57\% and 100.41\%, respectively.

</details>


### [217] [The Influence of Fraudulent AI-Generated Responses on Software Engineering Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23805)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过二次分析四个软件工程调查数据集，发现AI辅助参与对定量分析结果影响较小，但对定性分析结果影响较大，强调了在开放性问题调查中结合多种验证程序的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）的出现引发了关于软件工程调查中欺诈性或AI辅助参与的新担忧，可能影响调查结果的有效性。

Method: 对四个软件工程调查数据集进行二次分析，采用人工识别可疑回答、自动化AI生成文本检测、描述性统计分析和主题分析，比较原始数据集与人工清理后的数据集之间的差异。

Result: 定量结果在过滤可疑回答后总体保持稳定，但部分人口统计和分析变量出现中等程度变化；定性结果受框架变化、代码显著性和证据性质影响更大。

Conclusion: AI辅助参与对软件工程调查的影响因分析类型而异，建议在依赖开放性问题的研究中结合多种验证程序以确保结果有效性。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce new concerns regarding fraudulent or AI assisted participation in software engineering surveys. Aims: This study investigates how suspicious or potentially AI assisted responses may affect the validity of software engineering survey findings. Method: We conducted a secondary analysis of four software engineering survey datasets using manual identification of suspicious responses, automated AI generated text detection, descriptive statistical analysis, and thematic analysis. We compared findings obtained from the original and manually cleaned datasets. Results: Quantitative findings generally remained stable after filtering suspicious responses, although some demographic and analytical variables showed moderate variation, affecting the interpretation of specific participant groups and contextual characteristics. In contrast, qualitative findings were more strongly influenced by changes in contextual framing, code prominence, and the nature of the evidence supporting interpretation, shaping how participants' experiences and study contexts were interpreted and characterized. Conclusions: AI assisted participation may influence software engineering survey findings differently depending on the type of analysis being conducted. The findings reinforce the importance of combining multiple validation procedures, particularly in studies relying on open ended responses.

</details>


### [218] [RESTOR: Automated Test Oracle Generation for RESTful APIs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23963)
